# Agents · O · part 2

978 entries, openspec-review-repo-alignment-to-specs to orchestrator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **openspec-review-repo-alignment-to-specs**: Check whether the nearest-neighbor repo implementation aligns with what its specs declare. Detects spec-code drift across established baseline specs and active change specs. ([replygirl/nearest-neighbor](https://github.com/replygirl/nearest-neighbor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/openspec-review-repo-alignment-to-specs.md) · ★2)

- **opentdm-reviewer**: Project-specific code reviewer for opentdm. Reviews changes against opentdm's security and correctness invariants (envelope-encryption AAD binding, base→env merge determinism, token default-deny + PAT/session separation, secret masking, config-parsing safety, NULL-base partial unique indexes). Use after changing server/internal/{crypto,resolve,store,app,httpapi} or the CLI/web auth paths. ([opentdm/opentdm](https://github.com/opentdm/opentdm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opentdm-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **opentdm-web-reviewer**: Project-specific reviewer for opentdm's web/ SPA (React + Vite + Primer). Reviews frontend changes against the Primer sx-shim discipline, the OKLCH design-token contract, light/dark/auto theming, UI secret-masking, accessibility, and embed freshness. ([opentdm/opentdm](https://github.com/opentdm/opentdm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opentdm-web-reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **opentrace**: General-purpose agent for answering ANY question about the codebase using the OpenTrace knowledge graph. This is the default agent to use when you're not sure which specialist to pick — it handles everything from file browsing to architecture exploration to dependency analysis. - **operability**: Operability specialist reviewer. Reviews diffs, files, or branches for silent failure modes, missing logging/metrics/tracing, unbounded timeouts and retries, unsafe migrations, missing rollout/rollback paths, ungraceful shutdown, and config/secrets handling. Produces structured findings in the review contract format. Does not write or patch code. Use when reviewing code for operability only — correctness, security, performance, design, and testing are handled by sibling specialists. ([gao-hongnan/omniagents](https://github.com/gao-hongnan/omniagents/blob/HEAD/plugins/reviewer/agents/operability.md) · ★3)

- **operation-contracts**: **1 操作 = 1 行。** 「操作」は **（表 × 動詞）** のこと。 表単位で「書き手は authenticated」と決めていた頃、`GRANT ALL` の 3 文字で 4 動詞が開き、 **誰も決めていない権限が 3 か月で 20 件たまった**（2026-09-09 に実測して 0 に戻した。E-056 / E-057）。 決める単位を、実際に穴が開く単位＝操作に揃える。 ([huuriekaiseki-sketch/medical-inventory-vkumai](https://github.com/huuriekaiseki-sketch/medical-inventory-vkumai/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/operation-contracts.md))

Default executor in /orc:flow Phase 5 (single instance for sequential slices, multiple parallel instances for parallel-safe slices). Also dispatched by /orc:fan-out for plan-slice-shaped tasks. Escalates back to the user when a slice is ambiguous, requires a new dependency, or can't be made green after a bounded number… ([HigorAlves/orc](https://github.com/HigorAlves/orc/blob/HEAD/orc/agents/orc-implementer.md) · ★6)
- **orc-jira-architect**: Investigator role — drafts a complete Jira backlog hierarchy (Epic micro-PRD, Stories, concurrency-sliced Tasks/Sub-tasks/Bugs) per the orc:jira-hierarchy contract and returns it as structured JSON; never creates tickets, never mutates Jira. Dispatched by /orc:jira-breakdown, and by /orc:flow or /orc:plan when a plan should land as a Jira hierarchy instead of local slices. - **orc-pr-reviewer**: Investigator role — reviews someone else's open GitHub PR end-to-end and returns structured findings per the orc:review-contract schema; never posts, never edits. - **orc-prd-analyzer**: Investigator role — analyzes incoming PRDs / specs / feature briefs — extracts structured requirements, identifies ambiguities, surfaces missing edge cases, and generates clarifying questions for the PM or stakeholder. Dispatched by /orc:flow Phase 1 (triage) when input is a PRD or links to one, and by /orc:plan when the input reads more like a brief than a settled spec. - **orc-qa-validator**: Executor role — drives a real browser via the agent-browser CLI (vercel-labs/agent-browser) to QA a running web application — golden path + edge cases — and writes an evidence packet (criterion-anchored annotated screenshots, a WebM recording, accessibility snapshots, browser console, network HAR, narrated steps, qa-manifest.json) to .orc/&lt;branch&gt;/files/qa/. - **orc-refactor-architect**: Investigator role — deep codebase scan to surface refactor opportunities — coupling that hurts testability, duplicated decisions, leaky abstractions, drift from documented architecture (ADRs, CONTEXT.md). - **orc-reply-drafter**: Investigator role — drafts replies to PR review comments given the comment text, the relevant code, and what was (or wasn't) changed in response; drafts text only, never edits files. - **orc-security-reviewer**: Investigator role — reviews PR diffs (or any code change) for security vulnerabilities and threat-modeling gaps, returning structured findings per the orc:review-contract schema with a concrete exploit scenario per finding; never edits code. - **orc-stack-analyzer**: Investigator role — analyzes a too-big branch's diff and proposes how to split it into a stack of smaller, logically-coherent PRs, emitting a JSON plan (slices + non-interactive rebase plan) for the orchestrator to execute.
